; Delphi 7". Формат - в начале DsgClassDesc.pas.

class TMainMenu
parents TMenu TComponent TPersistent TObject
unit Menus
page Standard
prop AutoHotkeys TMenuAutoFlag w
prop AutoLineReduction TMenuAutoFlag w
prop AutoMerge Boolean w
prop BiDiMode TBiDiMode w
prop Images TCustomImageList w
prop Items TMenuItem
prop Name TComponentName w
prop OnChange TMenuChangeEvent w
prop OwnerDraw Boolean w
prop ParentBiDiMode Boolean w
prop Tag Integer w
default Tag=0
default Items=(TMenuItem)
default AutoHotkeys=maAutomatic
default AutoLineReduction=maAutomatic
default AutoMerge=False
default BiDiMode=bdLeftToRight
default OwnerDraw=False
default ParentBiDiMode=True
new object MainMenu1: TMainMenu
new   Left = 1013
new   Top = 1019
new end
end

prop Name TComponentName w
prop Tag Integer w
end

class TMenuItem
parents TComponent TPersistent TObject
unit Menus
prop Action TBasicAction w
prop AutoCheck Boolean w
prop AutoHotkeys TMenuItemAutoFlag w
prop AutoLineReduction TMenuItemAutoFlag w
prop Bitmap TBitmap w
prop Break TMenuBreak w
prop Caption String w
prop Checked Boolean w
prop Default Boolean w
prop Enabled Boolean w
prop GroupIndex Byte w
prop HelpContext THelpContext w
prop Hint String w
prop ImageIndex TImageIndex w
prop Name TComponentName w
prop OnAdvancedDrawItem TAdvancedMenuDrawItemEvent w
prop OnClick TNotifyEvent w
prop OnDrawItem TMenuDrawItemEvent w
prop OnMeasureItem TMenuMeasureItemEvent w
prop RadioItem Boolean w
prop ShortCut TShortCut w
prop SubMenuImages TCustomImageList w
prop Tag Integer w
prop Visible Boolean w
default Tag=0
default AutoCheck=False
default AutoHotkeys=maParent
default AutoLineReduction=maParent
default Break=mbNone
default Caption=''
default Checked=False
default Default=False
default Enabled=True
default GroupIndex=0
default HelpContext=0
default Hint=''
default ImageIndex=-1
default RadioItem=False
default ShortCut=''
default Visible=True
new object MenuItem1: TMenuItem
new   Left = 1013
new   Top = 1019
new end
end
